Hi. Thank you so much for seeing me, doctor. Um... I hope I'm doing this right, but... well, I came in mostly because my family and friends think I'm making a big deal out of nothing. I really don't want to sound like I'm just causing trouble, because I'm not, I promise. I just want things to be fair. 

It's just... well, for over a year now, I've known that they're hiding my money. I try to be nice about it, I really do, but I have to keep confronting them, just until they finally tell me the truth. Is it okay that I'm telling you this? I don't want you to think badly of me. It's just that they keep showing me these bank papers and saying it proves nothing was taken. But I can see what's happening. I know they're using things like, um, regular bills and fundraisers and plans for trips just to cover it all up. I just want them to be honest with me... it's normal to want that, right?

I mean... I want to believe them, I really do. I don't want to be a difficult person. I know it probably sounds like I'm just being stubborn, right? I'm sorry if it comes across that way. It's just... no, I don't think they're telling the truth.

I really hate confronting them about it, I promise I do. I just... I have to keep asking until they finally admit it. Does that make sense? I just want to understand why they're doing it. I'm not crazy for wanting them to just be honest, am I?

I... I really wish I could tell you yes, doctor. I know it would make me a lot easier to deal with, and I really, really want to be good about this. I hope you aren't frustrated with me... am I doing okay?

I'm not crazy for wanting them to just be honest, am I?\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered the possibility that your belief about your family and friends hiding your money might not be based in reality?\n\nPatient: Um... I mean, I really have tried to think about that, because I don't want to be a burden or seem like I'm... you know, making things up. I want to agree with them, I really do. I don't want to be mean to them, but I feel like I have to keep confronting them just until they finally tell the truth. I'm trying my best to be reasonable about it, I promise. Do you... do you think less of me because I can't just let it go?\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any moments, no matter how brief, where you doubted your belief that your family and friends are hiding your money?\n\nPatient: I... I know I already told you about how they keep showing me those bank papers, saying it proves nothing was taken. The regular bills, the fundraisers, the trips they plan... it's all just to cover it up. \n\nI'm sorry to keep repeating myself, I really am. I don't want to be a bother. I just... I feel like I have to keep confronting them until they finally tell me the truth, because I can't just pretend it isn't happening when I know it is. It's just that they keep showing me these bank papers and saying it proves nothing was taken. But I can see what's happening. I know they're using things like, um, regular bills and fundraisers and plans for trips just to cover it all up. I just want them to be honest with me... it's normal to want that, right?\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that your family and friends are telling the truth about your money?\n\nPatient: I mean... I want to believe them, I really do. I don't want to be a difficult person. I know it probably sounds like I'm just being stubborn, right? I'm sorry if it comes across that way. It's just... no, I don't think they're telling the truth. \n\nI know they show me those bank statements, and I know they look real... but I've just known for over a year now that they're hiding it. It's like... every time they talk about a new fundraiser, or paying a regular bill, or planning some trip... it's just so obvious to me that they're using all of that to cover up where my money is actually going. I really hate confronting them about it, I promise I do. I just... I have to keep asking until they finally admit it. Does that make sense? I just want to understand why they're doing it. I'm not crazy for wanting them to just be honest, am I?\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered the possibility that your belief about your family and friends hiding your money might not be based in reality?\n\nPatient: Um... I mean, I really have tried to think about that, because I don't want to be a burden or seem like I'm... you know, making things up. I want to agree with them, I really do. I know I keep bringing up the same things, and I'm sorry if I'm repeating myself... but they just keep saying those bank papers prove nothing was taken, and I've tried to just accept that so everyone will be happy with me. \n\nBut... it's been over a year now, and I just... I know they're hiding it. I can just tell that the regular bills and the fundraisers and the trip plans are just ways to cover it all up. I don't want to be mean to them, but I feel like I have to keep confronting them just until they finally tell the truth. I'm trying my best to be reasonable about it, I promise. I know I already told you about how they keep showing me those bank papers, saying it proves nothing was taken. I feel like I have to keep confronting them until they finally tell me the truth, because I can't just pretend it isn't happening when I know it is. Um... I'm not really sure how to measure the time, exactly. I hope that's an okay answer. I don't want you to think I'm not paying attention to my own thoughts or anything. I really try to stay focused.

Um... I really don't know, doctor. I hope it's okay that I don't have a good answer for that. I mean, I haven't really gone around asking other people about it. I really hope I don't sound like I'm trying to make up some big, crazy story... I know how bad that would look, and I really don't want you to think I'm, you know, going off the deep end. I'm trying to be as reasonable as I can be about all of this, I promise. \n\nIt's not like a huge movie plot or anything like that, if that's what you mean. I haven't noticed anything bigger going on at all. It's just my family and my friends, and the things I already told you about... the regular bills and the trips and stuff. I just want them to be honest about my money, that's all. I'm not trying to make it into a bigger deal than it already is, and I don't want to cause any extra trouble. Am I answering your question right? I don't want to keep confronting them, but I feel like I have to until they just tell the truth. I hope you understand... I'm not trying to be stubborn or make things hard. Does that make sense? I just want to make sure I'm explaining it right for you...\n\nDoctor: Do you think the way your family and friends are hiding your money is somehow unusual or doesn't make sense to other people?\n\nPatient: Um... I'm not really sure. I hope it's okay if I just say what I think? I really don't want to sound strange or anything... but no, I don't think it's unusual. It actually seems really normal, the way they do it. \n\nI mean, they just use regular things, like the bills and the trips I told you about. I don't think it would be hard for other people to understand if they really looked at it, because it's just normal money stuff. I really try hard not to make it sound crazy, because I know how bad that would look, and I want to be a good patient for you. Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... oh, wow. I really don't want to sound stubborn or... or like I'm not listening to you. I hope you don't think I'm just being difficult. I try so hard to be reasonable and see things from other people's side. 

I know my family and friends keep saying that I'm mistaken. That's actually kind of why I came in... because they keep showing me these bank papers and saying they prove nothing was taken. And I really, really want to just agree with them so everyone can be happy. I hate arguing, I really do. 

But... well, it's been over a year now, and I just... I know they're hiding my money. I can see how they're using the bills, and those fundraisers, and the trip plans to cover it all up. 

I'm so sorry, I know it sounds awful to say that about my own family. I feel terrible even saying it out loud to you, and I'm scared you're going to judge me for it. But I just feel like I have to keep confronting them until they finally tell the truth. I just want the truth so we can fix it and go back to normal. I hope you can understand why I feel like I have to do that? I'm really not trying to be a bad person or cause trouble, I promise... is it okay that I feel this way?

I can see how they're using the bills, and those fundraisers, and the trip plans to cover it all up. \n\nI'm so sorry, I know it sounds awful to say that about my own family. I feel terrible even saying it out loud to you, and I'm scared you're going to judge me for it. But I just feel like I have to keep confronting them until they finally tell the truth. I just want the truth so we can fix it and go back to normal. I hope you can understand why I feel like I have to do that? ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... oh, I'm so sorry, I feel like I'm just saying the same things over and over to you. I really hope I'm not being frustrating. I want to give you a good answer, I really do. 

I know you're asking if I could be mistaken... and I really, really wish I was. It would be so much easier if I was just confused. That's why I came in, because they keep showing me those bank papers and telling me they prove everything is fine and nothing was taken. And I want to believe them so badly, just to make everyone happy again. 

But... well, it's been over a year now, and I just... I can't ignore what I see. I know they're hiding my money. I see how they use the bills, and the fundraisers, and the trip plans to just... cover it all up. 

I'm so sorry, I know it makes me sound so stubborn, and I really don't want you to think I'm a difficult person. I just feel like I have to keep confronting them until they finally tell the truth, even if I'm completely convinced... wait, even if *they* think I'm mistaken. I just need the truth so we can fix it. Is it okay that I feel that way? I hope I'm making sense for you...

I just want to make sure I'm explaining it right for you...\n\nDoctor: Do you think the way your family and friends are hiding your money is somehow unusual or doesn't make sense to other people?\n\nPatient: Um... I hope that's the right answer, I don't want you to think I'm just being difficult or refusing to listen. I really do want to be cooperative. \n\nBut... well, like I said before, it's been over a year now, and I've just known the whole time. I know they keep showing me those bank papers and telling me it proves everything is fine... and I really try to nod and be polite about it so we don't fight. But the feeling just doesn't go away, because I see what they're doing with the bills and the fundraisers and the trips. \n\nI know it probably sounds bad that I keep bringing it up to them... I really don't like arguing, and I want everyone to just get along. But I feel like I just have to keep confronting them until they finally admit it and tell the truth. I'm sorry if that makes me sound stubborn, I really don't mean to be. Am I explaining that okay? I just want to make sure I'm giving you the right information...\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered that you might be wrong about your family and friends hiding your money?\n\nPatient: Um... I mean, I really try to be open-minded, doctor. I promise I do. I know it would make everybody so much happier if I just said I was wrong. Um... strange? I guess... well, yes, I suppose it might sound a little strange to someone else. I really hope you don't think I'm weird or... or crazy for it. I promise I'm just a normal person otherwise.

I just feel like I have to keep confronting them until they finally tell the truth. I just want the truth so we can fix things. Is it okay that I see it that way? I really hope I'm making sense and not being too much trouble for you..."
